## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- mlflow-operator is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 3 CRD(s)\: MLflowOperator, MLflow, MLflowConfig.
- deploying 1 workload(s) (mlflow-operator-controller-manager) with 1 container(s).
- watching 15 resource type(s).
- exposing 1 service(s) on ports\: 8443.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 8080.
- protected by 1 network policy/policies.
- connecting to external services\: mlflow.
- runtime dependencies\: MLflow, MySQL, PostgreSQL, mlflow-artifacts.
- defining 11 API spec type(s) across 25 struct(s) with 114 total fields.
- with 113 operator config constants.
- reporting 4 status condition types.
- with 1 ingress route(s).
- Python k8s client with 15 API call(s).
- integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations.
- kustomize overlays\: config/admission, config/base, config/crd, config/manager, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/openshift, config/overlays/rhoai, config/rbac.
- references components\: mlflow (import).
